A new halt slug with no matching row fails the suite loudly.</p>\n    </section>\n\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"scenario-catalogue\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">08</span><h2>Scenario catalogue (initial coverage)</h2></header>\n      <p>Grouped by area; one test function each. Target end-state, not a single slice — slices below build the infra and add scenarios incrementally.</p>\n      <p>Scenarios that spawn <code>claude -p</code> carry <code>@pytest.mark.real_claude</code> (excluded from the default <code>pytest</code> run; opt-in via <code>-m real_claude</code>). Scenarios that halt pre-spawn run on every default invocation.</p>\n\n      <p><strong>Best case</strong> <em>(real_claude)</em></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>3-issue dependency-ordered drain reaches Done in order; runlog totals positive and sum across issues; child MCP calls include <code>save_issue</code> (transition to Done) and <code>save_comment</code> (summary) per issue</li>\n        <li>Mixed Todo + Backlog states picked up; correct planning</li>\n        <li>Single-issue drain</li>\n        <li>Empty cycle exits cleanly <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Per-issue halts</strong> (<code>orchestrator._drain_one_issue</code> paths)</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>Missing <code>repo:</code> label → halt + reason <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n        <li><code>repo:</code> label resolves to nonexistent path → halt <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n        <li>Worktree <code>add</code> fails (dirty tree) → halt <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n        <li><code>set_state</code> (Todo→In-Progress) fails with HTTP 500 → halt <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n        <li>Child exits without setting Done <em>(real_claude; coerced via <code>--max-turns 1</code>)</em> → halt + reason matches <code>final_linear_state</code></li>\n        <li>Child exits non-zero <em>(real_claude; provoked by an invalid prompt or a tool that errors)</em> → halt</li>\n        <li>Per-issue token cap exceeded mid-stream <em>(real_claude; verbose prompt + <code>per_issue_tokens=1000</code>)</em> → breach kill; runlog records breach scope/metric/limit/observed</li>\n        <li>Per-issue time cap exceeded <em>(real_claude; prompt \"wait 60s\" + <code>per_issue_seconds=10</code>)</em> → time breach recorded</li>\n        <li>Revert <code>set_state</code> (In-Progress→Todo) fails after a halt → halt-reason concatenates <em>(real_claude or pre-spawn)</em></li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Cycle-level halts</strong></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>DependencyCycleError</code> from <code>pending_issues</code> → halt before any issue attempted; <code>cycle_halt_reason</code> set; runlog otherwise empty <em>(no claude spawned)</em></li>\n        <li>After two Done issues, cycle-cap breach detected → third issue not attempted; halt reason names cumulative metric <em>(real_claude)</em></li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Resume</strong> <em>(real_claude)</em></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>Run halts on issue 2; re-run with the same cycle picks up issue 2 (mock has it back at Todo via the revert) and issue 3; second runlog file separate from first; final mock state correct</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Session / usage tracking</strong> <em>(real_claude; predicate assertions on numbers)</em></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>Single-issue drain produces <code>usage.cumulative.input_tokens &gt; 0</code>, <code>usage.cumulative.output_tokens &gt; 0</code>, <code>peak_context &gt; 0</code>, <code>cost_usd &gt; 0</code>, <code>num_turns &gt;= 1</code>, <code>session_id</code> non-empty, <code>is_error == false</code>, <code>model</code> non-empty</li>\n        <li>Multi-issue drain: cycle aggregates (<code>cycle_tokens_cumulative</code>, <code>cycle_cost_usd</code>, <code>cycle_duration_seconds</code>) equal the sum of per-issue values</li>\n        <li>Worker dedup invariant (<code>worker.py:416</code>) verified indirectly: re-emit a non-deterministic prompt and confirm <code>usage.cumulative</code> is monotonically non-decreasing within a turn; explicit dedup behaviour stays unit-tested</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Linear edge cases</strong> <em>(no claude spawned — parent halts before pre-spawn <code>set_state</code> succeeds)</em></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>500, 429, GraphQL error, malformed JSON, slow-within-timeout (drain proceeds), slow-beyond-timeout (halts)</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>Out of scope for MVP</strong></p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>Honeycomb/OTel span assertions (separate slice — needs a fake OTLP collector; production code wires spans cleanly per <code>drain_cycle/telemetry.py</code>)</li>\n        <li>True MCP-server fake for children (scripted-child + direct GraphQL POST satisfies the contract)</li>\n      </ul>\n\n      <p><strong>In MVP, bounded</strong> — slice 4 ships one subprocess smoke test that spawns the real <code>drain-cycle</code> binary against the mock and asserts exit code plus runlog presence. This single test covers <code>cli.py</code>'s secrets-load order and process exit-code path. Larger subprocess scenarios stay out of scope.</p>\n\n      <p><strong>Note on the worker <code>_UsageAccumulator</code> dedup invariant</strong> — the worker dedupes by <code>message.id</code> (<code>worker.py:416</code>). Real claude rarely emits duplicate ids in practice, so the dedup branch is exercised by existing unit tests, not by integration scenarios. Plan accordingly: keep the dedup unit test (<code>test_usage_accumulator_dedup.py</code> or equivalent); the integration suite asserts on monotonicity, not on dedup directly.</p>\n    </section>\n\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"slices\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">09</span><h2>Slices (verifiable build order)</h2></header>\n      <p>Each slice ships in one session and ends with a passing scenario.</p>\n      <ol>\n        <li><strong>Production seam + ADR</strong> — <code>LINEAR_API_URL</code> env var in <code>linear.py</code>; unit test confirms override; default unchanged. Runlog <code>HOME</code> re-resolution verified (grep + accessor if needed). <strong>Deliverable</strong>: <code>docs/adrs/0001-integration-test-architecture.md</code> recording the chosen approach, rejected alternatives, and revisit conditions.</li>\n        <li><strong><code>MockLinear</code> skeleton</strong> — FastAPI app with <code>current_cycle</code> and <code>pending_issues</code> only; serves a hard-coded scenario; one unit test wires through <code>linear.py</code> and confirms the call lands on the mock. Harness sets <code>OTEL_SDK_DISABLED=true</code>.</li>\n        <li><strong><code>linear_mcp_shim</code> + first real-claude drain</strong> — stdio MCP server implementing <code>save_issue</code> and <code>save_comment</code> against the mock; harness writes a <code>.mcp.json</code> registering it; first <strong>real-claude</strong> happy-path drain of one issue reaches Done end-to-end. Schemas captured from the official Linear MCP. Marked <code>@pytest.mark.real_claude</code>.</li>\n        <li><strong><code>IntegrationHarness</code> fixture + subprocess smoke</strong> — tmpdir HOME, repos.yml, real git repo, <code>.mcp.json</code> writer, mock startup. Slice 3's scenario refactored onto the harness. <strong>Plus</strong>: one subprocess test spawns <code>drain-cycle</code> against the mock end-to-end and asserts exit code plus runlog file existence.</li>\n        <li><strong>Mock GraphQL completeness + drift guard</strong> — add <code>get_issue</code>, <code>set_state</code>, WorkflowState resolution; happy-path drain of 3 ordered issues passes. <strong>Plus</strong>: <code>test_linear_surface_audit</code> fails when any <code>_post(operation=...)</code> call site in <code>linear.py</code> lacks a mock handler.</li>\n        <li><strong>Fault injection</strong> — per-operation, per-call-index fault modes in the mock; scenario covers <code>set_state</code> HTTP 500 halt (pre-spawn, fast). Per-scenario contract enforced (runlog assertions, mock state, call sequence).</li>\n        <li><strong>Pre-spawn halt scenarios</strong> — repo-resolution failures, worktree-add failure, set_state 500. Fast, no claude.</li>\n        <li><strong>Real-claude halt scenarios</strong> — child exits without Done (via <code>--max-turns 1</code>); child non-zero exit (via invalid prompt). Marked <code>real_claude</code>.</li>\n        <li><strong>Breach scenarios</strong> — time-breach first (tight <code>per_issue_seconds</code> plus \"wait 60s\" prompt — robust). Token-breach gated on the slice-8 reproducibility spike. Both <code>real_claude</code>.</li>\n        <li><strong>Cycle-level halt scenarios</strong> — <code>DependencyCycleError</code> (no claude); cycle-cap breach after two Done issues (<code>real_claude</code>).</li>\n        <li><strong>Resume scenario</strong> — drive a halt, re-invoke orchestrator on the same mock cycle, assert second runlog file and final state. <code>real_claude</code>.</li>\n        <li><strong>Usage-tracking + Linear edge-case scenarios</strong> — usage predicates on a happy-path drain (<code>real_claude</code>); 429 / malformed / slow-timeout halts on the parent (no claude).</li>\n      </ol>\n      <p>Slices 1–4 are the foundation; 5–12 are independent and ship in any order once 4 lands. Each slice opens a Linear issue under the initiative and closes per <code>AGENTS.md</code> (review → fix → commit + push → Linear summary comment → Done).</p>\n    </section>\n\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"critical-files\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">10</span><h2>Critical files</h2></header>\n      <p>To modify:</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>drain_cycle/linear.py</code> — env-var override for GraphQL URL (slice 1)</li>\n        <li><code>drain_cycle/runlog.py</code> — verify <code>HOME</code> re-resolution; add small accessor only if cached at module load (slice 1)</li>\n      </ul>\n      <p>To create:</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>docs/adrs/0001-integration-test-architecture.md</code> — chosen approach + rejected alternatives + revisit conditions (slice 1)</li>\n        <li><code>tests/integration/conftest.py</code> — harness fixture, <code>real_claude</code> marker config</li>\n        <li><code>tests/integration/mock_linear.py</code> — FastAPI GraphQL server + shared in-memory store + fault injection</li>\n        <li><code>tests/integration/linear_mcp_shim.py</code> — stdio MCP server backed by the shared store</li>\n        <li><code>tests/integration/prompts/*.md</code> — issue-body prompts (happy_path, exit_without_done, verbose, wait, etc.)</li>\n        <li><code>tests/integration/test_happy_path.py</code>, <code>test_pre_spawn_halts.py</code>, <code>test_real_claude_halts.py</code>, <code>test_breach.py</code>, <code>test_cycle_halts.py</code>, <code>test_resume.py</code>, <code>test_usage_tracking.py</code>, <code>test_linear_edges.py</code>, <code>test_linear_surface_audit.py</code>, <code>test_subprocess_smoke.py</code> — scenarios</li>\n      </ul>\n      <p>To reuse from existing tests:</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li>Real git-repo setup pattern in <code>tests/test_orchestrator_iteration.py:22-90</code></li>\n        <li>Linear fixture style in <code>tests/test_linear_pending_issues.py:65-70</code></li>\n        <li><code>_CLAUDE_CMD</code> patch point at <code>drain_cycle/orchestrator.py:25</code></li>\n      </ul>\n    </section>\n\n    <section class=\"s\" id=\"verification\">\n      <header><span class=\"ix\">11</span><h2>Verification</h2></header>\n      <p>End-to-end:</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>uv run pytest tests/integration/ -v</code> — non-<code>real_claude</code> scenarios green; no real Linear network calls; no real <code>LINEAR_API_KEY</code> leaking in. The default local invocation.</li>\n        <li><code>uv run pytest tests/integration/ -m real_claude -v</code> — full suite green against a real Anthropic API key. Run on demand; instructions live in <code>tests/integration/README.md</code>.</li>\n        <li><code>uv run pytest --cov=drain_cycle/orchestrator --cov=drain_cycle/worker --cov=drain_cycle/runlog tests/integration/</code> — coverage on orchestrator, worker, and runlog rises meaningfully.</li>\n        <li>Each slice closes per <code>AGENTS.md</code>: review → fix → commit + push to main → Linear summary comment → Done.</li>\n      </ul>\n      <p><strong>Halt-coverage assertion</strong> (enforces KR 2):</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>test_halt_coverage</code> greps <code>mark_error</code> slugs from <code>drain_cycle/**.py</code>, collects the union of <code>exception.slug</code> attributes (or <code>halt_reason</code> strings) observed across the full integration run (including <code>real_claude</code>), and asserts equality. Fails locally when a new halt slug lands in production code without a covering scenario.</li>\n      </ul>\n      <p>Negative checks during build:</p>\n      <ul>\n        <li><code>grep -r \"api.linear.app\" tests/</code> returns nothing — no accidental real-Linear calls in fixtures.</li>\n        <li>The mock's call log (parent GraphQL + child MCP) records every operation a scenario expects; an unexpected call fails the test loudly.</li>\n        <li><code>test_linear_surface_audit</code> fails when any <code>_post(operation=...)</code> call site in <code>linear.py</code> lacks a mock handler.</li>\n        <li>Cost budget guard: the <code>real_claude</code> suite prints total Anthropic spend at the end of each run; manual review flags runs over $5 (catch a runaway loop scenario early).</li>\n      </ul>\n    </section>\n  </main>\n\n  <footer>Rendered from <code>docs/integration-test-architecture.md</code> on 2026-05-28 · regenerate to update.</footer>\n</div>\n\n<script>\n  const links = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('aside nav a'));\n  const targets = links.map(a => document.getElementById(a.getAttribute('href').slice(1))).filter(Boolean);\n  const byId = new Map(targets.map((el, i) => [el.id, links[i]]));\n  const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {\n    for (const e of entries) {\n      if (e.isIntersecting) {\n        links.forEach(a => a.classList.remove('active'));\n        byId.get(e.target.id)?.classList.add('active');\n      }\n    }\n  }, { rootMargin: '-30% 0px -60% 0px' });\n  targets.forEach(t => io.observe(t));\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
